# Graph Report - thingtime  (2026-09-05)

## Corpus Check
- 1762 files · ~7,339,476 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 36542 nodes · 90874 edges · 1614 communities (914 shown, 700 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 98% EXTRACTED · 2% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 2248 edges (avg confidence: 0.66)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `1d727389`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
